# Muscle & Physique - Full Body A/B
_Your Blaze Camp plan_

- **Main goal:** Build muscle
- Muscle & physique
- **Level:** Advanced (L3): 3 years of consistent training, training 4 days a week now
- **Plan type:** 12 weeks (recommended) · Week 1-12
- **Start date:** 2026-10-12
- **Training days per week:** 4 · Monday, Tuesday, Thursday, Friday · Session length (minutes): 75
- Release accepted (Liability release (client), English)

## Before you start

- Your schedule cannot fit 10 weekly sets for Chest, Back (lats / upper back), Biceps, Triceps: add a training day
- Starting sets were lowered for Quads, Hamstrings, Glutes, Chest, Back (lats / upper back), Side delts, Biceps, Triceps, Calves so the stairway fits your 75-minute sessions.

**Your schedule options**
- Keep 75 minutes: lower starting sets, same stairway (this plan)
- One more training day (5 days)

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

Planned effort across the stairway: weeks 1-6: leave 3 in the tank; weeks 7-9: leave 2 in the tank; weeks 10-11: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 12: leave 1 in the tank. The last week may add one intensifier on a small-muscle exercise.

How heavy? Each exercise tells you how many reps to leave in the tank: "leave 2 in the tank" means you could do 2 more reps at the end of the set (about RPE 8; 3 left = RPE 7, 1 left = RPE 9). The first weeks leave more in the tank, the last weeks less. Week 1: start a little lighter, with a weight you could lift 2-3 more times.

Your plan climbs like a stairway: small steps first, a bit bigger after the halfway week, the hardest weeks at the end.

## Week 1 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 70 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Reverse Lunge with Medball Held Forward**
   - Swap for: Medball Reverse Lunge with Reach Down Over the Front Leg / Medball Forward Lunge with Medball Held Forward / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Overhead Reach
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Reverse%20Lunge%20with%20Medball%20Held%20Forward)

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-60 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Optional: add a core superset to any exercise
   - Swap for: Crunch / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / V-Up
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Reverse Crunch
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/L1rt7qr5LCk)

**Strength**
1. **Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)** — 3 × 3-6 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Reps capped for this exercise (3-6): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Front Squat (barbell) / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Box%20Squat%20-%20any%20variation%2C%20dead-stop%20('Deload'%20%3D%20sit%20and%20unload%20on%20the%20box))
2. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Swap for: Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Machine Row (Pronated Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip))
3. **Walking Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm Lat Pulldown
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat / Reverse Lunge / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Walking%20Lunge)
4. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Walking Lunge
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XbZgoSNJXm4)

**Accessories**
1. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
2. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)
   - Swap for: Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
3. **Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly
   - Swap for: Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aTYlqC_JacQ)
4. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - Swap for: Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish) / Face Pull / Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Reverse%20Fly%20(rear-delt%20pec%20deck))
5. **Dumbbell Concentration Curl** — 2 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cable Curl (Zone 1 / Zone 2 / Zone 3 set-ups) / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Reverse-Grip (Pronated) EZ-Bar Curl
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Concentration%20Curl)
6. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic A (blue): 15 Sec Run/ 45 Sec Walk** — Blue (A): short / high intensity · 15-20 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20A%20(blue)%3A%2015%20Sec%20Run%2F%2045%20Sec%20Walk)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

### Thursday · A2 - leg press + pull
_about 74 min_

**Warm-up**

**Core**

**Strength**
1. **Leg Press (machine)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   > Constrained, no axial (spinal) load: the default second squat-pattern lift of the week (PER-04).
   - Swap for: Low DB Squat (two DBs hanging at the sides) / High-Zercher DB Squat (DB hugged high at chest) / Sumo Squat (DB or KB) / Goblet Squat (KB/DB)

2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Press (machine)
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set

3. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)

   - Swap for: Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)

4. **Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Side (Lateral) Lunge
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Close Grip)

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Floor%20Prone%20Y%20%2F%20T%20Raises%20(bodyweight%20or%20light%20DBs))
4. **Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted)** — 3 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Barbell Calf Raise / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)

## Week 2 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2.6 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on DB Lateral (Side) Raise (Thursday)
- Quads is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Hamstrings is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Glutes is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Biceps is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Triceps is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Calves is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Core**
1. **Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch / Crunch
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gq7ToKnOC1Q)

**Accessories**
1. **Hip Thrust (machine) (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Focus slot for your priority muscle
   - Swap for: Reverse Hyperextension / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Cable Pull-Through / Sandbag Hip Thrust
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hip%20Thrust%20(machine))
2. **Reverse Hyperextension (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Focus slot for your priority muscle
   > Use if available and the client feels the right muscles.
   - Swap for: Cable Pull-Through / Dumbbell Hip Thrust / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/3vmbvoT2m-U)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic C (orange): 5 Minute Run/ :30 Sec Walk** — Orange (C): long / easy · 30-40 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - This cardio session is part of the plan even if you skip the optional lifting.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20C%20(orange)%3A%205%20Minute%20Run%2F%20%3A30%20Sec%20Walk)

### Thursday · A2 - leg press + pull
_about 76 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Front-Hold Squat (ball held out in front)**
   - Swap for: Medball Forward Lunge with Medball Hug / Medball Forward Lunge with Cross-Body Turn / Medball Forward Lunge with Overhead Reach
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Front-Hold%20Squat%20(ball%20held%20out%20in%20front))

**Core**
1. **Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / V-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Crunch)
2. **Reverse Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Leg Raise (lying / supine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Reverse%20Crunch)

**Strength**
1. **Leg Press (machine)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row
   > Constrained, no axial (spinal) load: the default second squat-pattern lift of the week (PER-04).
   - Swap for: Low DB Squat (two DBs hanging at the sides) / High-Zercher DB Squat (DB hugged high at chest) / Sumo Squat (DB or KB) / Goblet Squat (KB/DB)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Press%20(machine))
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Leg Press (machine)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Bent-Over Barbell Row / Bent-Over Barbell Row (Supinated / Yates grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/zKWe7UAWbIo)
4. **Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Side (Lateral) Lunge
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Close Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lat%20Pulldown%20(Supinated%20%2F%20Underhand%20Grip))

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
3. **Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs)** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair
   - Swap for: Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Face Pull / Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Floor%20Prone%20Y%20%2F%20T%20Raises%20(bodyweight%20or%20light%20DBs))
4. **Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted)** — 3 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Barbell Calf Raise / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Deficit%20Calf%20Raise%20(weighted))
5. **DB Lateral (Side) Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Seated DB Lateral Raise / Cable Side Raise (leaning) / Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / DB Upright Row
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OuG1smZTsQQ)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic C (orange): 8 Minute Run/ 1 Minute Walk** — Orange (C): long / easy · 30-40 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20C%20(orange)%3A%208%20Minute%20Run%2F%201%20Minute%20Walk)

**Cool-down**

## Week 3 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 3.2 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on DB Lateral (Side) Raise (Thursday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 70 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Reverse Lunge with Medball Held Forward**
   - Swap for: Medball Reverse Lunge with Reach Down Over the Front Leg / Medball Forward Lunge with Medball Held Forward / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Overhead Reach

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-60 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Crunch / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / V-Up
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Reverse Crunch

**Strength**
1. **Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)** — 3 × 3-6 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)
   - Reps capped for this exercise (3-6): it is harder than its group.

2. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)
   - Swap for: Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Machine Row (Pronated Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip))
3. **Walking Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm Lat Pulldown
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat / Reverse Lunge / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Walking%20Lunge)
4. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Walking Lunge
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XbZgoSNJXm4)

**Accessories**
1. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
2. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)
   - Swap for: Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
3. **Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly
   - Swap for: Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aTYlqC_JacQ)
4. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - Swap for: Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish) / Face Pull / Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull

   - Swap for: Cable Curl (Zone 1 / Zone 2 / Zone 3 set-ups) / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Reverse-Grip (Pronated) EZ-Bar Curl
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Concentration%20Curl)
6. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise)

## Week 4 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 3.8 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

## Week 5 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 4.4 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- New bonus exercise: Barbell Upright Row (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

## Week 6 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 5 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Cable Fly (Friday)
- Chest is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

## Week 7 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 6 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Barbell Upright Row (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

- +1 set on Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (Thursday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

   - Swap for: Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Dumbbell Concentration Curl

   - Swap for: Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)

   - Swap for: Seated DB Lateral Raise / Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / Cable Side Raise (leaning) / DB Lateral (Side) Raise

## Week 9 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 8 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- +1 set on DB Lateral (Side) Raise (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 78 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Reverse Lunge with Medball Held Forward**
   - Swap for: Medball Reverse Lunge with Reach Down Over the Front Leg / Medball Forward Lunge with Medball Held Forward / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Overhead Reach

   - Swap for: Crunch / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / V-Up

   - per side = 1 set

   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Machine Row (Pronated Grip)

## Week 10 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 9 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on DB Lateral (Side) Raise (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 79 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Reverse Lunge with Medball Held Forward**
   - Swap for: Medball Reverse Lunge with Reach Down Over the Front Leg / Medball Forward Lunge with Medball Held Forward / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Overhead Reach

1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-60 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Crunch / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / V-Up
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wq6N_PjU5fA)
2. **Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Reverse Crunch
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/L1rt7qr5LCk)

**Strength**
1. **Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)** — 3 × 3-6 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)
   - Reps capped for this exercise (3-6): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Zercher Squat (barbell) / Front Squat (barbell) / High-Bar Back Squat / High Zercher Barbell Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Box%20Squat%20-%20any%20variation%2C%20dead-stop%20('Deload'%20%3D%20sit%20and%20unload%20on%20the%20box))
2. **Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box)
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Machine Row (Pronated Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20Barbell%20(Supinated%20Grip))
3. **Walking Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm Lat Pulldown
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat / Reverse Lunge / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Walking%20Lunge)
4. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Walking Lunge
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) / Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XbZgoSNJXm4)

**Accessories**
1. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
2. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)** — 3 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Reverse%20Fly%20(rear-delt%20pec%20deck))
3. **Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)
   - Swap for: Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl / Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aTYlqC_JacQ)
4. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Dumbbell Concentration Curl
   - Swap for: Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
5. **Dumbbell Concentration Curl** — 2 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cross-Body Dumbbell Hammer Curl / Cable Curl (Zone 1 / Zone 2 / Zone 3 set-ups) / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / Seated Dumbbell Curl
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Concentration%20Curl)
6. **Barbell Calf Raise** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise)
7. **DB Lateral (Side) Raise (bonus)** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · last set MAX (technical failure) · 60-90 s rest
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Seated DB Lateral Raise / Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / Cable Side Raise (leaning) / DB Upright Row
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OuG1smZTsQQ)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic A (blue): 15 Sec Run/ 45 Sec Walk** — Blue (A): short / high intensity · 15-20 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20A%20(blue)%3A%2015%20Sec%20Run%2F%2045%20Sec%20Walk)

## Week 11 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 10 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New bonus exercise: Lateral Raise - 'Spill the Can' (thumbs down, internally rotated) (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 82 min_

**Warm-up**

## Week 12 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 11 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

_last week: intensifiers allowed (MYO set on the last machine / isolation exercise)_

**What changes this week**
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball FFE Split Squat/Lunge with Medball Held Forward**
   - Swap for: Medball Reverse Lunge with Forward Reach / Medball Reverse Lunge with Overhead Reach / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Forward Reach
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20FFE%20Split%20Squat%2FLunge%20with%20Medball%20Held%20Forward)

**Core**
1. **Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Crunch / Sit-Up / Side Plank (elbow)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gq7ToKnOC1Q)
2. **Leg Raise (lying / supine)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Reverse Crunch / Copenhagen Plank / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) / Side Plank Press (advanced: star side plank press)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/VpEcj96HO4k)

**Strength**
1. **Cable Side Raise (leaning)** — 4 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Seated DB Lateral Raise / Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / DB Upright Row / DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lq7eLC30b9w)
2. **Barbell Deadlift (conventional)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Swap for: Wide-Grip Barbell Deadlift / Sumo Deadlift / Barbell Good Morning / Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/op9kVnSso6Q)
3. **Alternating DB Floor Press** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / Incline Barbell Bench Press / Alternating Flat DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20DB%20Floor%20Press)
4. **Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-on-Wall%20DB%20Deadlift%20(RDL))
5. **Barbell Push Jerk** — 2 × 1-5 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 3-6 min rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (1-5): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press / Barbell Z-Press (seated on floor) / High-Incline Seated DB Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Push%20Jerk)

**Accessories**
1. **Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Nordic Hamstring Curl / Seated Leg Curl (machine) / Lying Leg Curl (machine) / Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Leg%20Curl%20(seated%20or%20lying))
2. **Cable Fly** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Fly)
3. **Machine Calf Raise** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · last set MAX (technical failure) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise)
4. **Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot on floor) (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Focus slot for your priority muscle
   - Swap for: Reverse Hyperextension / Hip Thrust (machine) / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Barbell Hip Thrust
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tiZWu8faIkM)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

### Saturday · Optional day (optional)
_about 43 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio

**Core**
1. **Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Reverse Crunch / Sit-Up / Leg Raise (lying / supine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Crunch)

**Accessories**
1. **Cable Side Raise (leaning) (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Focus slot for your priority muscle
   - Swap for: Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / Seated DB Lateral Raise / DB Upright Row / DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lq7eLC30b9w)
2. **DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - Focus slot for your priority muscle
   - Swap for: Seated DB Lateral Raise / Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / DB Upright Row / DB Lateral (Side) Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=DB%20Lateral%20Raise%20(Supinated%20%2F%20palms-up%20grip))

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic B (green): 3 Minute Run/ 3 Min Walk** — Green (B): moderate · 20-30 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - This cardio session is part of the plan even if you skip the optional lifting.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20B%20(green)%3A%203%20Minute%20Run%2F%203%20Min%20Walk)

- ⚠ Two-leg / single-leg share follows the plan (V28, 1)
